                            d = d * (1 - gam)
                    defined = 'flow' in hist
                    for m in MFAM:
                        c = m([z1, z2])
                        st1 = dict(z=[c, c], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                        st_end, Q1 = jia_run(st1, hist, P)
                        Y1 = share_of(Q1)
                        B = B_value(Y0, Y1)
                        n += 1
                        if not defined:
                            viol += (B != 'undef-reading')
                        else:
                            viol += (B != pred_true)
                        viol += (st_end['z'][0] != st_end['z'][1])
    nw = vw = 0
    for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
        for _ in range(6):
            L = rng.randint(1, 6)
            hist = [rng.choice(['drive', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
            st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], R=[F(100), F(100)])
            _, y0, _ = wu_run(st0, hist)
            Y0 = share_of(y0)
            d = z1 - z2
            pred = False
            for a in hist:
                if a == 'drive':
                    pred = pred or d != 0
                    d = d * (1 + WU['eta'] * WU['beta'] - WU['gam'])
                else:
                    d = d * (1 - WU['gam'])
            for m in MFAM:
                st1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
                _, y1, _ = wu_run(st1, hist)
                B = B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))
                nw += 1
                vw += (B != ('undef-reading' if 'drive' not in hist else pred))
    return n, viol, nw, vw

def subsegments(n_beats, k):
    size = n_beats // k
    return [list(range(i * size, (i + 1) * size)) for i in range(k)]

def relations_jia(label, st0, hist, brk_sched=None, P=JIA, k=3, eps_d=F(1, 100), m=m_mean, q=0):
    """在族甲（或族甲⁺）上求 D、B_m（份额）、B_m^q、F(q)、Ω_W(q)、Bias(q 对另一路)。
    申报：子段 k 等分；Pass = 导通拍数（Qᵢ > 0）；熟径常走判据与 Ω 复走判据同取 Pass ≥ k；Ω 第三项为各子段都有导通拍；
    Brk_W(q) = 1 当且仅当 W 内某拍 brk_q = 1；Bias：(4a) W 起点 z_q − z_o > ε_δ，(4b) 每个子段 q 的流量份额 > 1/2（长窗稳定偏续的操作化）。"""
    o = 1 - q
    segs = subsegments(len(hist), k)
    st, Qs = jia_run(dict(st0), hist, P, brk_sched)
    Y0 = share_of(Qs)
    stI, c, ext = Im_jia(st0, m)
    _, QsI = jia_run(stI, hist, P, brk_sched)
    B = B_value(Y0, share_of(QsI))
    D = st0['z'][0] != st0['z'][1]
    passes = sum(1 for Q in Qs if Q[q] > 0)
    fam = st0['z'][q] > 0 and passes >= k
    # F：逐子段比较任务 T_k，起点状态取实际运行
    Fok = fam
    cur = dict(st0)
    seg_lead = []
    for sg in segs:
        sub = [hist[t] for t in sg]
        sub_brk = [brk_sched[t] for t in sg] if brk_sched else None
        _, Qk = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
        Yk = share_of(Qk)
        cI, _, _ = Im_jia(cur, m)
        _, QkI = jia_run(cI, sub, P, sub_brk)
        YkI = share_of(QkI)
        ld = lead('share', Yk, q + 1)
        bq = Bq_value('share', Yk, YkI, q + 1)
        seg_lead.append((ld, bq))
        Fok = Fok and ld == '独先' and bq is True
        cur, _ = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
    brkq = any(b[q] for b in brk_sched) if brk_sched else False
    seg_pass = all(any(Qs[t][q] > 0 for t in sg) for sg in segs)
    Om = passes >= k and not brkq and seg_pass
    a4 = st0['z'][q] - st0['z'][o] > eps_d
    b4 = all(sum((Qs[t][q] for t in sg), F(0)) > sum((Qs[t][o] for t in sg), F(0)) for sg in segs)
    Bias = a4 and b4
    return dict(label=label, D=D, B=B, F=Fok, Omega=Om, Bias=Bias, a4=a4, b4=b4, fam=fam, seg=seg_lead, Y0=Y0)

def main():
    print('一、族甲、族戊的 I_m 实现（路径层模型甲：路径 = 通道／突触，Depth = zᵢ）')
    st = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], L=[F(1, 5), F(0)])          # 《场具化构造式》H1 驱动拍之后的状态（5.1）
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, ext = Im_jia(st, m)
        print(f'  族甲 z = (21/10, 1/10)，{m.__name__}：c* = {c}，回写唯一（z₁ = z₂ = c*），库存 L 冻结；与 B_phys 外交换结构物质 {ext}（正为加入）')
    print('  族甲物理实现：《场具化构造式》申报的界外作用只有向通道 1 定量投放 2 A_u（C5）；I_m 一般需要「加入或取出任意量」的界外设定操作，须另申报（扩）；')
    print('  申报后质量账以界外交换项闭合，c* ∈ [min, max] ⊂ [0, z_max]，不越适用域。')
    st = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], R=[F(19), F(39)])           # 《场具化构造式》H1 族戊：池 40 → 20 → 19；突触 2 驱动一拍写 1/10、池 39
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st, m, 'pool')
        s2, _, feas2 = Im_wu(st, m, 'frozen')
        print(f'  族戊 z = (21/10, 1/10)，R = (19, 39)，{m.__name__}：c* = {c}；R-池随动后 R = ({s1["R"][0]}, {s1["R"][1]})，R-池资源可行 {feas}；池冻结读法资源可行 {feas2}')
    st = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], R=[F(20), F(5)])
    print('  族戊见证（形式良定而 R-池资源不可行）：z = (2, 0)，R = (20, 5)：')
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4))):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st, m, 'pool')
        print(f'    {m.__name__}：c* = {c}，R′ = ({s1["R"][0]}, {s1["R"][1]})，R-池资源可行 {feas}')
    print('    R-池资源可行条件（定理 W-2；不表示已有合法的物理设定途径，后者须申报扩充操作）：c* ≤ min_i [zᵢ + g₁Rᵢ]；此处 c* ≤ 0 + (1/10)·5 = 1/2，即 m_α 中 α ≤ 1/4')
    print('二、定理 J-1 / W-1（份额读数）的有限核验')
    n, viol, nw, vw = thmJ1_check()
    print(f'  族甲（κ_构 ∈ {{0, 1/2, 1}}，γ ∈ {{0, 1/10, 1/2, 1}}，z 对 36，随机 flow/rest 史 6 条，m 5 个）：判值 {n}，违例 {viol}')
    print(f'  族戊（z 对 36，随机 drive/rest 史 6 条，m 5 个）：判值 {nw}，违例 {vw}')
    print('三、族甲上 D 成立而 B_m 不成立的两种来源')
    hist = ['flow'] * 3
    st0 = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0), F(0)])
    P0 = dict(JIA, kap=F(0))
    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, P0); _, Q1 = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, P0)
    print('  律级（κ_构 = 0，导通不读迹应）：Y =', fmt(share_of(Q0)), '→', fmt(share_of(Q1)), '；B_mean =', B_value(share_of(Q0), share_of(Q1)))
    brk = [(0, 1)] * 3
    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, JIA, brk)
    res = {}
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        _, Q1 = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m)[0], hist, JIA, brk)
        res[m.__name__] = B_value(share_of(Q0), share_of(Q1))
    print('  态级（扩：通道 2 阻断，冻结）：Y =', fmt(share_of(Q0)), '；B(min/mean/max) =', res)
    # 区分：改变冻结量（解除阻断）后 B 恢复；律级在任何冻结量取值下 B 都为假
    _, Q0u = jia_run(st0, hist, JIA); _, Q1u = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, JIA)
    print('  区分检验（不是 I_m，是对冻结量的另一反事实）：解除阻断后 B_mean =', B_value(share_of(Q0u), share_of(Q1u)),
          '；κ_构 = 0 时对一切 brk ∈ {0,1}² 的 B_mean =',
          [B_value(share_of(jia_run(st0, hist, P0, [b] * 3)[1]), share_of(jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, P0, [b] * 3)[1]))
           for b in ((0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0))])
    print('四、族甲、族戊上 D、B_m、F、Ω_W、Bias 的情形表（m = mean，子段 3，ε_δ = 1/100；q = 通道／突触 1）')
    cases = [
        ('甲1 基准（κ_构 = 1，z = (2, 0)，flow×6）', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
        ('甲2 律级退化（κ_构 = 0）', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, dict(JIA, kap=F(0))),
        ('甲3 扩：通道 2 全窗阻断', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, [(0, 1)] * 6, JIA),
        ('甲4 扩：通道 1 末拍被截断', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, [(0, 0)] * 5 + [(1, 0)], JIA),
        ('甲5 等深（z = (1, 1)）', dict(z=[F(1), F(1)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
        ('甲6 单次导通（flow, rest×5）', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] + ['rest'] * 5, None, JIA),
        ('甲7 迹应在阈下（z = (1/200, 0)，ε_δ = 1/100）', dict(z=[F(1, 200), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
    ]
    for lab, st0, hist, brk, P in cases:
        r = relations_jia(lab, st0, hist, brk, P)
        print(f'  {lab}：D={r["D"]} B={r["B"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]}（4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}）熟径={r["fam"]}；子段 (Lead, B^q) = {r["seg"]}')
    print('  族戊（发放率，份额读数，与族甲同一参数化编码，《场具化构造式》命题五）：')
    st0 = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], R=[F(20), F(40)])
    hist = ['drive'] * 6
    _, y0, ok0 = wu_run(dict(st0), hist)
    Y0 = share_of(y0)
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
        _, y1, ok1 = wu_run(s1, hist)
        print(f'    戊1 z = (2, 0)，drive×6，{m.__name__}：Y = {fmt(Y0)} → {fmt(share_of(y1))}，B = {B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))}，I_m 的 R-池资源可行 {feas}，W 内收支 {ok1}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.7　agg12.py　定理 AGG：线性聚合的投影与切锥判据、瓶颈逐位判据（10.2）

~~~python
"""聚合模型乙下 I_m 回写的有定义性与唯一性（正文定理 AGG）。
运行：python3 agg12.py
线性聚合：回写集 S = {z′ ∈ ℚ^{X_K}：A z′ = c*·1，ℓ ≤ z′ ≤ h}。
  判据（一）S 为单点 ⇔ 每个坐标在 S 上的投影为单点（Fourier–Motzkin 精确投影）。
  判据（二）设 z₀ ∈ S。S = {z₀} ⇔ ker A ∩ T(z₀) = {0}，T(z₀) 为盒在 z₀ 处的切锥（z₀ₓ = ℓ 处 dₓ ≥ 0，z₀ₓ = h 处 dₓ ≤ 0）。
  推论：c* 严格居于 (ℓ, h) 且 z₀ = c*·1 可行（均值型）时，T = 全空间，唯一 ⇔ rank A = |X_K|。
瓶颈聚合（Depth = 沿径最小）：S = {z′ ∈ [c*, h]^{X_K}：每路至少一位 = c*}；
  S 为单点 ⇔ c* = h，或不存在「其所在各路长都 ≥ 2」的位（即每一位都独占某条单点路）。
程序同时以子集穷举复核瓶颈判据。精确有理运算，参数一律在 ℚ 中取。
"""
import sys, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
Z = F(0)

def rank(M):
    M = [list(r) for r in M]
    r = 0
    for c in range(len(M[0]) if M else 0):
        piv = next((i for i in range(r, len(M)) if M[i][c] != 0), None)
        if piv is None:
            continue
        M[r], M[piv] = M[piv], M[r]
        for i in range(len(M)):
            if i != r and M[i][c] != 0:
                f = M[i][c] / M[r][c]
                M[i] = [a - f * b for a, b in zip(M[i], M[r])]
        r += 1
    return r

def fm_project(cons, n, k):
    """cons：[(a, b)] 表示 a·z ≤ b。消去 k 以外的全部变量，返回 (可行?, 下界, 上界)（None 表示无界）。"""
    cur = [(list(a), b) for a, b in cons]
    for j in range(n):
        if j == k:
            continue
        P = [c for c in cur if c[0][j] > 0]
        N = [c for c in cur if c[0][j] < 0]
        new = [c for c in cur if c[0][j] == 0]
        for ap, bp in P:
            for an, bn in N:
                fp, fn = ap[j], -an[j]
                a = [fn * x + fp * y for x, y in zip(ap, an)]
                b = fn * bp + fp * bn
                new.append((a, b))
        # 规范化去重
        seen, cur = set(), []
        for a, b in new:
            s = next((abs(x) for x in a if x != 0), None)
            if s is None:
                if b < 0:
                    return (False, None, None)
                continue
            key = (tuple(x / s for x in a), b / s)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                cur.append((list(key[0]), key[1]))
    lo = hi = None
    for a, b in cur:
        if a[k] > 0:
            v = b / a[k]
            hi = v if hi is None else min(hi, v)
        elif a[k] < 0:
            v = b / a[k]
            lo = v if lo is None else max(lo, v)
    if lo is not None and hi is not None and lo > hi:
        return (False, lo, hi)
    return (True, lo, hi)

def poly(A, rhs, lo, hi):
    n = len(lo)
    cons = []
    for row, r in zip(A, rhs):
        cons.append((list(row), r))
        cons.append(([-x for x in row], -r))
    for i in range(n):
        e = [Z] * n; e[i] = F(1)
        cons.append((e, hi[i]))
        cons.append(([-x for x in e], -lo[i]))
    return cons

def linear_writeback(A, c, lo, hi, z0=None):
    n = len(lo)
    cons = poly(A, [c] * len(A), lo, hi)
    proj = []
    feas = True
    for k in range(n):
        ok, a, b = fm_project(cons, n, k)
        feas = feas and ok
        proj.append((a, b))
    unique = feas and all(a == b for a, b in proj)
    res = dict(feasible=feas, unique=unique, proj=[(str(a), str(b)) for a, b in proj])
    # 判据（二）：在一个已知可行点 z0 上检查 ker A ∩ 切锥
    if feas:
        if z0 is None:
            z0 = [a for a, b in proj] if unique else None
            if z0 is None:
                # 取可行点：逐坐标固定为投影下界后再投影（精确）
                z0 = []
                cc = list(cons)
                for k in range(n):
                    ok, a, b = fm_project(cc, n, k)
                    v = a if a is not None else (b if b is not None else Z)
                    z0.append(v)
                    e = [Z] * n; e[k] = F(1)
                    cc = cc + [(e, v), ([-x for x in e], -v)]
        dcons = poly(A, [Z] * len(A), [F(-1)] * n, [F(1)] * n)
        for i in range(n):
            e = [Z] * n; e[i] = F(1)
            if z0[i] == lo[i]:
                dcons.append(([-x for x in e], Z))
            if z0[i] == hi[i]:
                dcons.append((e, Z))
        nz = False
        for k in range(n):
            ok, a, b = fm_project(dcons, n, k)
            if ok and ((a is not None and a < 0) or (b is not None and b > 0)):
                nz = True
        res['cone_unique'] = not nz
        res['z0'] = z0
    else:
        res['cone_unique'] = None
    return res

def bottleneck_unique(paths, n, c, h):
    if c == h:
        return True
    for x in range(n):
        if all(len(p) >= 2 for p in paths if x in p):
            return False
    return True

def bottleneck_unique_brute(paths, n, c, h):
    if c == h:
        return True
    for k in range(1, n + 1):
        for R in itertools.combinations(range(n), k):
            if all(any(y not in R for y in p) for p in paths):
                return False
    return True

def random_linear_check(N, seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    n_ok = viol = nint = vint = 0
    for _ in range(N):
        n = rng.randint(2, 4)
        m = rng.randint(1, 3)
        A = [[F(rng.randint(0, 2), rng.choice([1, 2])) for _ in range(n)] for _ in range(m)]
        if any(all(x == 0 for x in r) for r in A):
            continue
        kind = rng.choice(['mean', 'raw'])
        if kind == 'mean':
            A = [[x / sum(r) for x in r] for r in A]
        lo, hi = [Z] * n, [F(1)] * n
        c = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        r = linear_writeback(A, c, lo, hi)
        if not r['feasible']:
            continue
        n_ok += 1
        viol += (r['unique'] != r['cone_unique'])
        if kind == 'mean' and 0 < c < 1:
            nint += 1
            vint += (r['unique'] != (rank(A) == n))
    return n_ok, viol, nint, vint

def random_bottleneck_check(N, seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    viol = 0
    for _ in range(N):
        n = rng.randint(1, 5)
        paths = []
        for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 5)):
            k = rng.randint(1, n)
            paths.append(tuple(sorted(rng.sample(range(n), k))))
        cov = set(x for p in paths for x in p)
        if cov != set(range(n)):
            continue
        h = F(1)
        c = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(1)])
        viol += (bottleneck_unique(paths, n, c, h) != bottleneck_unique_brute(paths, n, c, h))
    return N, viol

def main():
    z0 = F(219345, 524288)
    box = (F(-1, 4), F(7, 4))                     # SNN：z ∈ [lo − b, hi − b]
    print('一、s1 拍 26 各 K 的回写（线性聚合；盒为 z 的状态区间 [−1/4, 7/4]；c* ∈ [min, max] ∩ ℚ）')
    cases = [
        ('分叉 K，均值', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))], 'mean'),
        ('汇聚 K，均值', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((2, 0), (3, 0))], 'mean'),
        ('单突触 K，路径层', [((0, 0),), ((0, 1),)], 'mean'),
        ('嵌套 K = {路 A, 输入 0 支, 输入 1 支}，求和', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 0),), ((1, 0),)], 'sum'),
        ('分叉 K，求和', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))], 'sum'),
    ]
    z = {(0, 0): z0, (1, 0): z0}
    for name, paths, agg in cases:
        pos = sorted(set(x for p in paths for x in p))
        A = [[(F(1, len(p)) if agg == 'mean' else F(1)) if x in p else Z for x in pos] for p in paths]
        ds = [sum(A[k][pos.index(x)] * z.get(x, Z) for x in pos) for k in range(len(paths))]
        out = []
        for lab, c in (('min', min(ds)), ('mean', sum(ds) / len(ds)), ('max', max(ds))):
            r = linear_writeback(A, c, [box[0]] * len(pos), [box[1]] * len(pos))
            out.append(f'{lab}: c*={c} 有解={r["feasible"]} 唯一={r["unique"]} 切锥判据唯一={r["cone_unique"]}')
        print(f'  {name}：所经位 {len(pos)}，rank A = {rank(A)}；' + '；'.join(out))
    print('二、边界例与判据核验')
    A = [[F(1), Z, Z], [Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]]
    r = linear_writeback(A, Z, [Z] * 3, [F(1)] * 3)
    print(f'  线性：A = [(1,0,0),(0,1/2,1/2)]，c* = 0，盒 [0,1]³：投影 {r["proj"]}，唯一 {r["unique"]}，切锥判据 {r["cone_unique"]}，rank {rank(A)}')
    for c in (F(1, 2), F(1)):
        print(f'  瓶颈：K = {{x}},{{y}},{{x,y}}，c* = {c}：逐路判据唯一 {bottleneck_unique([(0,), (1,), (0, 1)], 2, c, F(1))}，穷举 {bottleneck_unique_brute([(0,), (1,), (0, 1)], 2, c, F(1))}')
    print('  瓶颈：s1 分叉 K（两路各两突触，不重叠），c* = mean：逐路判据唯一', bottleneck_unique([(0, 1), (2, 3)], 4, z0 / 2, box[1]))
    n, v, ni, vi = random_linear_check(2000, 20261102)
    print(f'  线性随机核验 {n} 例：判据（一）（二）一致违例 {v}；内部均值型 {ni} 例上与秩判据一致违例 {vi}')
    n, v = random_bottleneck_check(3000, 7)
    print(f'  瓶颈随机核验 {n} 例：逐路判据与穷举一致违例 {v}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.8　snn12.py　宿载甲：I\_m、合法域与参数机、定理 S-PAR、S-S1、S-FORK 及其推论一、S-INV、读数类型、F/Ω/Bias、定理 R-B、退化（10.3、10.6、10.9、10.10、10.13、11）

~~~python
"""宿载甲：冻结 SNN 实例 s1 上的 I_m、B_m、B_m^q、F、Ω_W、Bias 与退化机制。
运行：python3 snn12.py
R 层表示以第十一篇《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 为准：𝒦_g 定义 1—3、命题 1（冻结 SNN ∈ 𝒦_g）、R 层申报实例定义 4—7。
本程序 import 第十一篇附录程序 gate_class.py（载入前核 SHA-256），不另做编码；冻结 snn_core.py 只用于复现 s1 申报史与核对。
本篇新增的只有：抹平干预 I_m（K 内各突触 w := b + c*，其余状态冻结）及其重跑、三类读数、c* 参数精确求解（参数机）。
路径读法（正文 3.2）：
  分叉 fork：入口组 A_in = {输入 0, 1}（模式 A）；路 j = {w0j, w1j}，j = 0, 1；开放事件 = 拍内 s₀ = s₁ = 1 且 g_j = 1。
  汇聚 conv：路 A = {w00, w10}、路 B = {w20, w30}，都汇入输出 0；输出 0 的发放按该拍入口模式归属（只取份额读数）。
  单突触 single：路 j = {w0j}（路径层，与非 K 的 w1j 共同入口）——作为阈机制不同一的对照。
聚合模型乙：Depth(路) = 路内各突触 z 的算术平均。
回写规则：主规则 R-移——路 P 内每一突触平移同一量 c* − Depth(P)（等深时为恒等）；越出状态区间时 I_m 未定义。
  R-均（K 内每一突触都设为 z = c*）只在路内均匀（此时 R-均 = R-移）或 S-INV 前件成立时使用，定理 S-FORK 按 R-均 陈述。
参数 c 一律取 ℚ 中值；「c* ∈ [min, max]」均指 [min, max] ∩ ℚ。
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, importlib.util, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from common12 import (y_first, y_order, y_share, lead, B_value, Bq_value, m_min, m_max, m_mean, m_alpha, fmt)
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from deps12 import S, GC, H_CORE, H_GATE
# s1 申报史（与 snn_instance.py 第 7—11 行逐字相同）
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
WIN = [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('B', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 3
K_FORK = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))]
K_CONV = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((2, 0), (3, 0))]
K_SINGLE = [((0, 0),), ((0, 1),)]
A_IN = (0, 1)

def s1_state():
    st, logf = S.run(FORM)
    st, _ = S.run(REST, st=st)
    _, logw = S.run(WIN, st=st)
    assert S.log_hash(logf).startswith('707404eb6302adc8') and S.log_hash(logw).startswith('84d320db9a654be6')
    return (tuple(tuple(r) for r in st['w']), tuple(st['v']), tuple(st['x']), tuple(st['y']))

def as_lists(o):
    return ([list(r) for r in o[0]], list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3]))

def depths(G, o, K):
    w = o[0]
    return [sum((w[i][j] - G.b[i][j] for (i, j) in path), F(0)) / len(path) for path in K]

RULE = 'shift'

def shared_positions(K):
    seen = set()
    for path in K:
        for k in path:
            if k in seen:
                return True
            seen.add(k)
    return False

def legal_domain(G, o, K, rule=None):
    """回写规则下 I_c 有定义的 c 之集与 [min Depth, max Depth] 之交（闭区间，有理端点）；空则 None。
    R-移：∩_{P, x∈P} [lo_x − w_x + Depth(P), hi_x − w_x + Depth(P)]；R-均：∩_x [lo_x − b_x, hi_x − b_x]；
    R-移 遇共享结构位：返回 'reject'。"""
    rule = rule or RULE
    if rule == 'shift' and shared_positions(K):
        return 'reject'
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    L, U = min(ds), max(ds)
    for path, d in zip(K, ds):
        for (i, j) in path:
            if rule == 'shift':
                L = max(L, G.lo[i][j] - o[0][i][j] + d); U = min(U, G.hi[i][j] - o[0][i][j] + d)
            else:
                L = max(L, G.lo[i][j] - G.b[i][j]); U = min(U, G.hi[i][j] - G.b[i][j])
    return (L, U) if L <= U else None

def I_c(G, o, K, c, rule=None):
    """抹平干预。rule='shift'（R-移）：路内各突触平移 c − Depth(P)；rule='uniform'（R-均）：各突触设为 b + c。
    v、x、y、协议、开关、夹限冻结。越出 [lo, hi] 时返回 None（I_m 未定义）。"""
    rule = rule or RULE
    if rule == 'shift' and shared_positions(K):
        return None                                   # R-移 对共享结构位不适用：显式拒绝
    w = [list(r) for r in o[0]]
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    for path, d in zip(K, ds):
        for (i, j) in path:
            w[i][j] = (o[0][i][j] + (c - d)) if rule == 'shift' else (G.b[i][j] + c)
            if not (G.lo[i][j] <= w[i][j] <= G.hi[i][j]):
                return None
    return (w, list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3]))

def run_rec(G, o, hist, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """返回 (末态, 记录)；记录逐拍为 (入口向量 s, 门控 g)。"""
    o = as_lists(o)
    rec = []
    for sig in hist:
        o, r = G.step(o, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        rec.append((tuple(r['s']), tuple(r['g'])))
    return o, rec

# ---------- 读数 ----------
def times_counts(rec, reading):
    if reading == 'fork':
        tau, cnt = {0: None, 1: None}, {0: 0, 1: 0}
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec):
            if all(s[i] for i in A_IN):
                for j in (0, 1):
                    if g[j]:
                        cnt[j] += 1
                        if tau[j] is None:
                            tau[j] = t
        return tau, cnt
    if reading == 'single':                     # 路 j = 输入 0 → 输出 j；开放 = s₀ = 1 且 g_j = 1
        tau, cnt = {0: None, 1: None}, {0: 0, 1: 0}
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec):
            if s[0]:
                for j in (0, 1):
                    if g[j]:
                        cnt[j] += 1
                        if tau[j] is None:
                            tau[j] = t
        return tau, cnt
    if reading == 'conv':                       # 输出 0 的发放按入口模式归属；A、B 同拍（AB）不归属
        tau, cnt = {'A': None, 'B': None}, {'A': 0, 'B': 0}
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec):
            if g[0]:
                a, b = s[0] and s[1], s[2] and s[3]
                if a and not b:
                    lab = 'A'
                elif b and not a:
                    lab = 'B'
                else:
                    continue
                cnt[lab] += 1
                if tau[lab] is None:
                    tau[lab] = t
        return tau, cnt
    raise ValueError(reading)

def Ys(rec, reading):
    tau, cnt = times_counts(rec, reading)
    Y = {'share': y_share(cnt)}
    if reading != 'conv':                       # 汇聚读法下 C₌ 不成立，先后类读数不设（正文 3.2）
        Y['first'] = y_first(tau)
        Y['order'] = y_order(tau)
    return Y

# ---------- 参数机：c ∈ (L, U) 上的精确分段求解 ----------
def aff_eval(f, c):
    return f[0] + f[1] * c

def param_explore(G, o, K, hist, L, U, plastic=True, bap=True, rule=None):
    """K 内突触取 w = b + c（c 为参数），其余状态为数。返回 [(kind, lo, hi, rec)]：kind 'open' 表示开区间 (lo, hi) 上记录恒定，
    'point' 表示孤立点 c = lo。门控比较与夹限比较都是 c 的仿射函数与常数的比较，逐拍按根切分（正文定理 S-PAR）。"""
    rule = rule or RULE
    dom = legal_domain(G, o, K, rule)
    if dom in (None, 'reject') or not (dom[0] <= L and U <= dom[1]):
        raise ValueError('参数机只在回写合法域内运行：所给区间越出合法域')
    base = {}
    for path, d in zip(K, depths(G, o, K)):
        for (i, j) in path:
            base[(i, j)] = (o[0][i][j] - d) if rule == 'shift' else G.b[i][j]
    w = [[((base[(i, j)], F(1)) if (i, j) in base else (o[0][i][j], F(0))) for j in range(G.n_out)] for i in range(G.n_in)]
    v = [(F(vv), F(0)) for vv in o[1]]
    st = (w, v, list(o[2]), list(o[3]))
    out = []
    def concrete_from(st, t, c):
        wn = [[aff_eval(f, c) for f in row] for row in st[0]]
        vn = [aff_eval(f, c) for f in st[1]]
        _, rec = run_rec(G, (wn, vn, st[2], st[3]), hist[t:], plastic, bap)
        return rec
    def rec_(st, t, lo, hi, prefix):
        if t == len(hist):
            out.append(('open', lo, hi, prefix))
            return
        w, v, x, y = st
        name, I = hist[t]
        s = G.pat[name]
        I = [F(a) for a in I]
        vt = [(G.lam[j] * v[j][0] + sum(w[i][j][0] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j],
               G.lam[j] * v[j][1] + sum(w[i][j][1] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in))) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        comps = [(vt[j][0] - G.theta[j], vt[j][1]) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        # 夹限比较须先知门控，先按门控切分
        roots = sorted(set(-f[0] / f[1] for f in comps if f[1] != 0 and lo < -f[0] / f[1] < hi))
        if roots:
            pts = [lo] + roots + [hi]
            for r in roots:
                rc = concrete_from(st, t, r)
                out.append(('point', r, r, prefix + rc))
            for a, b in zip(pts[:-1], pts[1:]):
                rec_(st, t, a, b, prefix)
            return
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        g = [1 if aff_eval(vt[j], mid) >= G.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(G.n_out)]
        v2 = [((G.vreset[j], F(0)) if g[j] else vt[j]) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        x2 = [G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)]
        y2 = [G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        Ssum = [[None] * G.n_out for _ in range(G.n_in)]
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                pp = G.Ap * x2[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else F(0)
                pm = -G.Am * y2[j] if (plastic and bap and s[i]) else F(0)
                a0, a1 = w[i][j]
                if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                    a0, a1 = a0 - G.rho * (a0 - G.b[i][j]), a1 - G.rho * a1
                Ssum[i][j] = (a0 + pp + pm, a1)
        croots = set()
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                f = Ssum[i][j]
                if f[1] != 0:
                    for bound in (G.lo[i][j], G.hi[i][j]):
                        r = (bound - f[0]) / f[1]
                        if lo < r < hi:
                            croots.add(r)
        if croots:
            croots = sorted(croots)
            pts = [lo] + croots + [hi]
            for r in croots:
                out.append(('point', r, r, prefix + concrete_from(st, t, r)))
            for a, b in zip(pts[:-1], pts[1:]):
                rec_(st, t, a, b, prefix)
            return
        w2 = [[None] * G.n_out for _ in range(G.n_in)]
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                f = Ssum[i][j]
                val = aff_eval(f, mid)
                if val > G.hi[i][j]:
                    w2[i][j] = (G.hi[i][j], F(0))
                elif val < G.lo[i][j]:
                    w2[i][j] = (G.lo[i][j], F(0))
                else:
                    w2[i][j] = f
        rec_((w2, v2, x2, y2), t + 1, lo, hi, prefix + [(tuple(s), tuple(g))])
    rec_(st, 0, L, U, [])
    return out

def param_B(G, o, K, hist, reading, plastic=True, bap=True, q=None, verify=2, rng=None, rule=None):
    """对 c ∈ [min, max] ∩ ℚ（引理 M1′：恰为全体合法 m 的 c* 值域）求 B（或 B^q）的分段。
    先求回写合法域 D = legal_domain；D 外各段记 'undef-Im'（I_m 未定义，不给真假）；D 内用参数机精确分段。
    返回 (Y0, 段列表, 核验违例数)。段：(kind, a, b, val, Y1)，kind ∈ {'point', 'open'}。"""
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    lo, hi = min(ds), max(ds)
    _, rec0 = run_rec(G, o, hist, plastic, bap)
    Y0 = Ys(rec0, reading)
    dom = legal_domain(G, o, K, rule)
    pieces = []
    undef = {kk: 'undef-Im' for kk in Y0}
    def add_undef(kind, a, b):
        pieces.append((kind, a, b, dict(undef), None))
    if dom in (None, 'reject'):
        add_undef('point', lo, lo)
        if hi > lo:
            add_undef('open', lo, hi); add_undef('point', hi, hi)
        pieces.sort(key=lambda p: (p[1], 0 if p[0] == 'point' else 1))
        return Y0, pieces, 0
    L, U = dom
    if lo < L:
        add_undef('point', lo, lo); add_undef('open', lo, L)
    if U < hi:
        add_undef('open', U, hi); add_undef('point', hi, hi)
    if L == U:
        cand = [('point', L, L, run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, L, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1])]
    else:
        cand = [('point', L, L, run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, L, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1]),
                ('point', U, U, run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, U, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1])]
        cand += param_explore(G, o, K, hist, L, U, plastic, bap, rule)
    bad = 0
    rng = rng or random.Random(5)
    for kind, a, b, rec in cand:
        if kind == 'open':
            for _ in range(verify):
                c = a + (b - a) * F(rng.randint(1, 999), 1000)
                if run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, c, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1] != rec:
                    bad += 1
        Y1 = Ys(rec, reading)
        val = {}
        for kk in Y0:
            val[kk] = B_value(Y0[kk], Y1[kk]) if q is None else Bq_value(kk, Y0[kk], Y1[kk], q)
        pieces.append((kind, a, b, val, Y1))
    pieces.sort(key=lambda p: (p[1], 0 if p[0] == 'point' else 1))
    return Y0, pieces, bad

def piece_at(pieces, c):
    for kind, a, b, val, Y1 in pieces:
        if (kind == 'point' and c == a) or (kind == 'open' and a < c < b):
            return kind, a, b, val, Y1
    return None

def summarize(pieces, key):
    vals = [p[3][key] for p in pieces]
    return dict(all_true=all(v is True for v in vals), all_false=all(v is False for v in vals),
                undef=any(v == 'undef-reading' for v in vals), undef_Im=any(v == 'undef-Im' for v in vals))

def show_pieces(pieces, keys, maxn=12):
    lines = []
    for kind, a, b, val, Y1 in pieces[:maxn]:
        rng_ = f'c*={a}' if kind == 'point' else f'c*∈({a}, {b})'
        lines.append('      ' + rng_ + '：' + ('I_m 未定义（回写越出合法域）' if Y1 is None else
                                         '；'.join(f'{k}: Y′={fmt(Y1[k])} B={val[k]}' for k in keys)))
    if len(pieces) > maxn:
        lines.append(f'      ……共 {len(pieces)} 段')
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# ---------- F、Ω_W、Bias ----------
def subsegs(n, k):
    sz = n // k
    return [(i * sz, (i + 1) * sz) for i in range(k)]

def FOB(G, o, K, hist, reading='fork', q=0, k=3, eps_d=F(1, 100), plastic=True, bap=True):
    """申报：子段 k 等分；Pass_W(路 q) = 开放事件数；熟径：路内各突触 z > 0（场篇第十一第 3 节）且 Pass ≥ k；Ω 复走判据同取 Pass ≥ k，
    Brk 在 𝒦_g 中恒为 0（无断连机制，突触身份持存，第十一篇 3.4 Ident），第三项为各子段都有开放事件；
    Bias：(4a) W 起点 Depth_q − Depth_o > ε_δ，(4b) 每个子段 q 的开放事件数多于另一路；
    F：熟径 ∧ 每个子段的比较任务 T_k（起点取实际运行状态，窗 = 该子段）中 Lead_q = 独先 ∧ 对一切合法 m B_m^q(T_k) 为真（参数机）。"""
    o_end, rec = run_rec(G, o, hist, plastic, bap)
    tau, cnt = times_counts(rec, reading)
    paths = sorted(cnt.keys(), key=str)
    qq, oo = paths[q], paths[1 - q]
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    D = ds[0] != ds[1]
    fam = all(o[0][i][j] - G.b[i][j] > 0 for (i, j) in K[q]) and cnt[qq] >= k
    segs = subsegs(len(hist), k)
    seg_ok, seg_info = True, []
    cur = as_lists(o)
    for (a, b) in segs:
        sub = hist[a:b]
        Y0, pieces, bad = param_B(G, cur, K, sub, reading, plastic, bap, q=qq)
        ld = lead('first' if reading != 'conv' else 'share', Y0['first' if reading != 'conv' else 'share'], qq)
        kk = 'first' if reading != 'conv' else 'share'
        allB = all(p[3][kk] is True for p in pieces)
        seg_info.append((ld, allB, bad))
        seg_ok = seg_ok and ld == '独先' and allB
        cur, _ = run_rec(G, cur, sub, plastic, bap)
    Fv = fam and seg_ok
    seg_pass = all(any(rec[t][1][qq if reading != 'conv' else 0] and all(rec[t][0][i] for i in A_IN) for t in range(a, b))
                   for (a, b) in segs) if reading == 'fork' else None
    Om = cnt[qq] >= k and seg_pass
    a4 = (ds[q] - ds[1 - q]) > eps_d
    def seg_cnt(a, b, p):
        _, c = times_counts(rec[a:b], reading)
        return c[p]
    b4 = all(seg_cnt(a, b, qq) > seg_cnt(a, b, oo) for (a, b) in segs)
    return dict(D=D, F=Fv, Omega=Om, Bias=a4 and b4, a4=a4, b4=b4, fam=fam, seg=seg_info, cnt=cnt)

# ---------- 主程序 ----------
def main():
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    o26 = s1_state()
    print('〇、版本与复现')
    print('  snn_core.py', H_CORE[:16], '…；第十一篇 gate_class.py', H_GATE[:16], '…（载入前核 SHA-256）')
    print('  s1 申报史复现：形成期日志指纹 707404eb6302adc8…、W 日志指纹 84d320db9a654be6…（与 s1 冻结日志的指纹一致）')
    n, ncl, nti, bad = GC.compare_run(S, G, FORM + REST + WIN)
    print(f'  第十一篇命题 1 的逐拍核对在 s1 全史上重做：{n} 拍，不一致 {len(bad)}')
    print('  拍 26（W 起点）状态：w =', [[str(c) for c in r] for r in o26[0]])
    print('                      v =', [str(c) for c in o26[1]], '；y =', [str(c) for c in o26[3]], '；x =', [str(c) for c in o26[2]])
    print('一、δ 的对应（Q1）：z = w − b；δmax ↔ hi − b = 7/4；下界 lo − b = −1/4（z < 0 无场篇第一对应，记）')
    o_end, rec = run_rec(G, o26, WIN)
    zmin = None
    oo = as_lists(o26)
    for sig in WIN:
        oo, _ = G.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
        zz = min(oo[0][i][j] - G.b[i][j] for i in range(4) for j in range(2))
        zmin = zz if zmin is None else min(zmin, zz)
    print('  s1 窗 W 内全部突触 z 的最小值 =', zmin, '（< 0 则记「z < 0 位」）')
    for K, nm in ((K_FORK, '分叉'), (K_CONV, '汇聚'), (K_SINGLE, '单突触')):
        print(f'  {nm} K 的路深（均值聚合）：', [str(d) for d in depths(G, o26, K)])
    print('二、阈机制同一性与 C₌ 的归约（Q2）')
    print('  分叉读法：两输出 λ = 3/4、θ = 1、复位 0 相同；未发放前 v_j(t) = λ^t v_j(0) + S_j·u_A(t) + R_j(t)，S_j = w0j + w1j；')
    print('  对称条件 SYM（v_j(0)、y_j(0) 相等，非 K 驱动对两输出相等）下，两路共享入口驱动 u_A，阈序由 S 单调给出（定理 S-FORK）。')
    print('  s1 在拍 26：v =', [str(c) for c in o26[1]], '，y =', [str(c) for c in o26[3]], '——SYM 不成立（浅路有初始膜电位，深路有残余后迹），')
    print('  按式篇第二十附件一第一节须「直接申报并核验所需的阈序」：')
    tau, cnt = times_counts(rec, 'fork')
    print('    实际 W：首开放拍（相对 W 起点）', tau, '；开放事件数', cnt, '；P₁₇ 型条件（深者开放拍上浅者门控实参 < θ）直接读数：',
          'out1 在拍 26 的门控实参 =', str(F(3, 4) * o26[1][1] + o26[0][0][1] + o26[0][1][1]))
    print('三、I_m 与现有干预（Q3）')
    ds = depths(G, o26, K_FORK)
    o_min = I_c(G, o26, K_FORK, min(ds))
    abl = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], list(o26[1]), list(o26[2]), list(o26[3]))
    print('  「W 起点全部权重复位到基线」与 I_min(K_分叉) 的状态是否逐分量相同：', abl == o_min,
          '；与 I_min(K_汇聚)：', abl == I_c(G, o26, K_CONV, min(depths(G, o26, K_CONV))))
    print('  （s1 拍 26 非 K 突触都在基线，且 K 内浅路深度为 0 = min，故二者在 s1 上数值重合；一般情形 min_K Depth > 0 时复位越出居间条件）')
    print('  切断反传、冻结可塑性：干预时刻 K 上 z 不变（只改过程律），不是 I_m。')
    print('四、s1 上的 B_m（实际 W，可塑性开；c* 遍历 [min, max] 的精确分段）')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    for K, reading, nm in ((K_FORK, 'fork', '分叉'), (K_CONV, 'conv', '汇聚'), (K_SINGLE, 'single', '单突触')):
        Y0, pieces, bad = param_B(G, o26, K, WIN, reading, rng=rng)
        keys = list(Y0.keys())
        print(f'  {nm}：原读数', '；'.join(f'{k}={fmt(Y0[k])}' for k in keys), f'；分段 {len(pieces)}，随机点复核违例 {bad}')
        print(show_pieces(pieces, keys, maxn=25))
        for k in keys:
            print(f'    {k}：对一切合法 m 为真 {summarize(pieces, k)["all_true"]}；为假 {summarize(pieces, k)["all_false"]}；含读数未定义 {summarize(pieces, k)["undef"]}')
        for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
            c = m(depths(G, o26, K))
            _, r1 = run_rec(G, I_c(G, o26, K, c), WIN)
            Y1 = Ys(r1, reading)
            print(f'    {m.__name__}（c* = {c}）：', '；'.join(f'{k}: B={B_value(Y0[k], Y1[k])}' for k in keys))
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_FORK, WIN, 'fork', q=1)
    print('  分叉 B^q（q = 浅路 1）分段：')
    print(show_pieces(pieces, ['first']))
    print('五、汇聚读法的回写不变性（定理 S-CONV）：同一路两突触之和固定、W 内无夹限时，输出 0 的全部事件与回写拆分无关')
    nsplit = ndiff = nclip = 0
    for trial in range(200):
        c = F(rng.randint(0, 1000), 1000) * depths(G, o26, K_CONV)[0]
        base = I_c(G, o26, K_CONV, c)
        _, recb = run_rec(G, base, WIN)
        for path in K_CONV:
            (i1, j1), (i2, j2) = path
            delta = F(rng.randint(-500, 500), 1000) * F(1, 2)
            w = [list(r) for r in base[0]]
            w[i1][j1] += delta; w[i2][j2] -= delta
            if not all(G.lo[i][j] <= w[i][j] <= G.hi[i][j] for i in range(4) for j in range(2)):
                continue
            o2 = (w, base[1], base[2], base[3])
            # 无夹限检查
            oo = as_lists(o2); clip = False
            for sig in WIN:
                oo, r = G.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
                clip = clip or any(r['comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for k in r['comp'])
            _, rec2 = run_rec(G, o2, WIN)
            nsplit += 1
            nclip += clip
            if not clip and [g for _, g in rec2] != [g for _, g in recb]:
                ndiff += 1
    print(f'  随机拆分 {nsplit} 组（其中 W 内出现夹限 {nclip} 组）；无夹限组中事件序列与 R-均 回写不同者 {ndiff}')
    # 夹限可破坏不变性的见证：路 A 和 = 3/4，均分 (3/8, 3/8) 与偏分 (0, 3/4)；拍 26 的抑制分项在 w00 = 0 处被夹限截去
    P3 = [('A', (0, 0))] * 3
    wa = [list(r) for r in o26[0]]; wa[0][0] = F(3, 8); wa[1][0] = F(3, 8)
    wb = [list(r) for r in o26[0]]; wb[0][0] = F(0); wb[1][0] = F(3, 4)
    _, r_a = run_rec(G, (wa, list(o26[1]), list(o26[2]), list(o26[3])), P3)
    _, r_b = run_rec(G, (wb, list(o26[1]), list(o26[2]), list(o26[3])), P3)
    print('  夹限见证：拍 26 状态、路 A 和 = 3/4，拆分 (3/8, 3/8) 与 (0, 3/4)，P = A×3：门控序列', [g for _, g in r_a], '对', [g for _, g in r_b],
          '——偏分一侧 w00 = 0 处抑制分项被夹限截去，两路之和分离，输出 0 在第 2 拍是否发放不同')
    print('六、读数类型不一致（Q4）')
    # (a) s1 单突触 K，m = max
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_SINGLE, WIN, 'single')
    c = max(depths(G, o26, K_SINGLE))
    _, r1 = run_rec(G, I_c(G, o26, K_SINGLE, c), WIN)
    Y1 = Ys(r1, 'single')
    print('  (a) s1、单突触 K、m = max：', '；'.join(f'{k}: {fmt(Y0[k])} → {fmt(Y1[k])}，B={B_value(Y0[k], Y1[k])}' for k in Y0))
    # (b)(c) 申报协议下的分叉对照（可塑性关，SYM 成立）
    def mk(S0, S1, wB=F(1, 4)):
        w = [[S0 / 2, S1 / 2], [S0 / 2, S1 / 2], [wB, wB], [wB, wB]]
        return (w, [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)] * 4, [F(0), F(0)])
    P_b = [('A', (0, 0))] * 3
    ob_ = mk(F(3, 5), F(11, 20))
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, ob_, K_FORK, P_b, 'fork', plastic=False)
    print('  (b) 分叉、可塑性关、S₀ = 3/5、S₁ = 11/20（每路两突触均分）、非 K 突触 1/4、v = y = 0，P = A×3：原', '；'.join(f'{k}={fmt(Y0[k])}' for k in Y0))
    print(show_pieces(pieces, list(Y0.keys())))
    P_c = [('AB', (0, 0))] + [('A', (0, 0))] * 5
    oc_ = mk(F(1), F(3, 5))
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, oc_, K_FORK, P_c, 'fork', plastic=False)
    print('  (c) 分叉、可塑性关、S₀ = 1、S₁ = 3/5、非 K 突触 1/4，P = AB, A×5：原', '；'.join(f'{k}={fmt(Y0[k])}' for k in Y0))
    print(show_pieces(pieces, list(Y0.keys())))
    print('七、定理 S-FORK 的有限核验（随机 𝒦_g 成员、ρ ∈ {0, 1/10, 1/3, 1/2, 1}、plastic、bap 随机（ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]）、SYM 状态（路内可不均）、随机协议；R-均 回写；')
    print('    首批开放读数 B ⇔ 原读数为单元集，且与 m 无关；含 D = 0 的情形）')
    nn = viol = skip = badtot = nD0 = 0
    n_eq = v_eq = 0
    rng2 = random.Random(37)
    perrho = {}
    for trial in range(400):
        lam = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        th = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2)])
        d = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)])
        Ap = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)]); Am = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
        full = lambda val: [[val] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 3), F(1, 2), F(1)])
        Gx = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [lam] * 2, [th] * 2, [F(0)] * 2, [d] * 4, [d] * 2, Ap, Am, rho, full(F(1, 4)), full(F(0)), full(F(2)))
        S0 = F(rng.randint(2, 30), 20); S1 = rng.choice([S0, F(rng.randint(2, 30), 20)])
        u0, u1 = F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20), F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20)
        wB2, wB3 = F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20), F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20)
        w = [[S0 / 2 + u0, S1 / 2 + u0], [S0 / 2 - u0, S1 / 2 - u0], [wB2, wB2], [wB3, wB3]]
        if not all(0 <= w[i][j] <= 2 for i in range(4) for j in range(2)):
            continue
        vv = F(rng.randint(0, 9), 10); yy = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        o = (w, [vv, vv], [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for _ in range(4)], [yy, yy])
        L = rng.randint(2, 8)
        P = [(rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([0, 0, F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(L)]
        plastic = rng.choice([True, False])
        bapv = rng.choice([True, False])
        _, rec0 = run_rec(Gx, o, P, plastic, bapv)
        # 前件 P-A：首个开放事件之前无非入口拍发放
        first = None
        okA = True
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec0):
            if any(g):
                if all(s[i] for i in A_IN):
                    break
                okA = False
                break
        if not okA:
            skip += 1
            continue
        # 前件 NC：首个开放事件之前 K 内无夹限事件（路内两突触不均时夹限可逆转路和之序，见正文定理 S-FORK 前件）
        oo = as_lists(o); clipK = False
        for sig in P:
            oo, r_ = Gx.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])), plastic=plastic, bap=bapv)
            clipK = clipK or any(r_['comp'][k][3] != r_['comp'][k][4] for path in K_FORK for k in path)
            if any(r_['g']):
                break
        if clipK:
            skip += 1
            continue
        Y0, pieces, bad = param_B(Gx, o, K_FORK, P, 'fork', plastic=plastic, bap=bapv, verify=1, rng=rng, rule='uniform')
        badtot += bad
        # 读数等价：合法回写 R-移 与辅助操作 R-均 在 c ∈ C_leg（R-移）、两次干预运行在首个开放事件前 K 内都无夹限（NC′）时，Y_first 相同
        dom_s = legal_domain(Gx, o, K_FORK, 'shift')
        if dom_s not in (None, 'reject'):
            for c in {dom_s[0], dom_s[1], dom_s[0] + (dom_s[1] - dom_s[0]) * F(rng2.randint(1, 99), 100)}:
                ys, ncp = [], True
                for rl in ('uniform', 'shift'):
                    oi = I_c(Gx, o, K_FORK, c, rl)
                    oo = as_lists(oi); rec_i = []
                    for sig in P:
                        oo, r_ = Gx.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])), plastic=plastic, bap=bapv)
                        rec_i.append((tuple(r_['s']), tuple(r_['g'])))
                        if not any(r_['g'][j] and all(r_['s'][i] for i in A_IN) for j in (0, 1)) and \
                                any(r_['comp'][k][3] != r_['comp'][k][4] for path in K_FORK for k in path):
                            ncp = False
                        if any(r_['g'][j] and all(r_['s'][i] for i in A_IN) for j in (0, 1)):
                            break
                    ys.append(Ys(run_rec(Gx, oi, P, plastic, bapv)[1], 'fork')['first'])
                if ncp:
                    n_eq += 1
                    v_eq += (ys[0] != ys[1])
        single = len(Y0['first']) == 1
        vals = set(p[3]['first'] for p in pieces)
        nn += 1
        keyc = ('p' if plastic else '-') + ('b' if bapv else '-')
        perrho[keyc] = perrho.get(keyc, 0) + 1
        if S0 == S1:
            nD0 += 1
        viol += not (len(vals) == 1 and vals.pop() == single)
    print(f'  有效情形 {nn}（按 plastic/bap：{perrho}；其中 D = 0 者 {nD0}）（跳过前件 P-A 或 NC 不成立 {skip}）；违例 {viol}；参数机随机点复核违例 {badtot}')
    print(f'  读数等价（合法回写 R-移 与辅助操作 R-均，c 取 R-移 合法域的两端与一个内点，NC′ 成立者）：比较 {n_eq} 次，首批开放读数不同者 {v_eq}')
    print('八、F、Ω_W、Bias（分叉读法，q = 路 0，子段 3 × 6 拍，ε_δ = 1/100；F 中 B^q 对一切合法 m 求值）')
    for plastic, nm in ((True, 's1 实际（可塑性开）'), (False, 's1 定权（可塑性关：无加深作用）')):
        r = FOB(G, o26, K_FORK, WIN, plastic=plastic)
        print(f'  {nm}：D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]}（4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}）熟径={r["fam"]} 开放事件 {r["cnt"]}；子段 (Lead, ∀m B^q, 复核违例) {r["seg"]}')
        if not plastic:
            _, rr = run_rec(G, o26, WIN, plastic=False)
            print('    定权运行中 K 上 z 全程不变（无 Dτ 分项）：', as_lists(o26)[0] == run_rec(G, o26, WIN, plastic=False)[0][0])
    o_eq = I_c(G, o26, K_FORK, max(depths(G, o26, K_FORK)))
    r = FOB(G, o_eq, K_FORK, WIN)
    print(f'  等深态（拍 26 施 I_max 后作为新起点）：D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]} 开放事件 {r["cnt"]}')
    r = FOB(G, o26, K_SINGLE, WIN, reading='single')
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_SINGLE, WIN, 'single')
    print(f'  单突触 K：D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Bias={r["Bias"]}（4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}）；B 在 c* = min 处：', pieces[0][3])
    W1 = [('A', (0, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 17
    r = FOB(G, o26, K_FORK, W1)
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_FORK, W1, 'fork')
    print(f'  单次输入（A 后静置 17 拍）：D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]}；B^first 对一切合法 m 为真 {summarize(pieces, "first")["all_true"]}')
    print('九、表示要求（Q6，定理 R-B）：实际见证历史全同而 B_m 不同')
    th_list = [F(1), F(3)]
    recs, Bs, Fs = [], [], []
    for th1 in th_list:
        Gx = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [F(1), th1], [F(0)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(0),
                     [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
        o, recA = run_rec(Gx, Gx.init(), FORM + REST)
        _, recW = run_rec(Gx, o, WIN)
        full_hist = []
        oo = Gx.init()
        for sig in FORM + REST + WIN:
            oo2, r_ = Gx.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
            full_hist.append((r_['g'], tuple(sorted((k, v[4]) for k, v in r_['comp'].items())), tuple(oo2[1]), tuple(oo2[2]), tuple(oo2[3])))
            oo = oo2
        recs.append(full_hist)
        Y0 = Ys(recW, 'fork')
        c = max(depths(Gx, o, K_FORK))
        _, r1 = run_rec(Gx, I_c(Gx, o, K_FORK, c), WIN)
        Bs.append(B_value(Y0['first'], Ys(r1, 'fork')['first']))
        # 逐子段 F 的直接计算（不由整窗 B 推出）
        cur = as_lists(o)
        segrows = []
        for (a, b) in subsegs(len(WIN), 3):
            sub = WIN[a:b]
            _, rk = run_rec(Gx, cur, sub)
            Yk = Ys(rk, 'fork')
            cm = max(depths(Gx, cur, K_FORK))
            _, rkI = run_rec(Gx, I_c(Gx, cur, K_FORK, cm), sub)
            bq_max = Bq_value('first', Yk['first'], Ys(rkI, 'fork')['first'], 0)
            _, pcs, _ = param_B(Gx, cur, K_FORK, sub, 'fork', q=0)
            segrows.append((lead('first', Yk['first'], 0), bq_max, all(pp[3]['first'] is True for pp in pcs)))
            cur, _ = run_rec(Gx, cur, sub)
        Fs.append((segrows, FOB(Gx, o, K_FORK, WIN)['F']))
    print('  G（θ₁ = 1）与 G′（θ₁ = 3）：除 M 外比较规格相同（K_分叉、P = s1 申报史、W、首批开放、m = max）；')
    print('  自初态起全史（形成、静置、W 共 44 拍）每拍门控、各突触后值、v、x、y 全同：', recs[0] == recs[1])
    print('  整窗 B_max^first：G =', Bs[0], '；G′ =', Bs[1])
    print('  逐子段 (Lead₀, m = max 的 B^q, 对一切合法 m 的 B^q)：G =', Fs[0][0], '；G′ =', Fs[1][0])
    print('  F(路 0;T,W)（对一切合法 m 口径）：G =', Fs[0][1], '；G′ =', Fs[1][1])
    print('十、退化机制')
    Gd = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [F(1)] * 2, [F(0)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 16), F(1, 16), F(0),
                 [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
    od, _ = run_rec(Gd, Gd.init(), FORM + REST)
    print('  (1) 增强 = 抑制（A₊ = A₋ = 1/16）：s1 形成史后 z_T =', [str(od[0][i][0] - F(1, 4)) for i in (0, 1)],
          '；分叉 K 路深', [str(d) for d in depths(Gd, od, K_FORK)], '→ D 为假，B_m、F 由命题 1 与 F ⇒ B 为假')
    cnt_ok = cnt_all = 0
    for trial in range(300):
        dd = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        A = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
        Gp = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [F(1)] * 2, [F(0)] * 2, [dd] * 4, [dd] * 2, A, A, F(0),
                     [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
        L = rng.randint(1, 15)
        P = [rng.choice([('A', (F(1), F(0))), ('none', (0, 0))]) for _ in range(L)]   # 教学电流 1 使输出 0 与模式 A 同拍发放
        o, rec_ = run_rec(Gp, Gp.init(), P)
        cof = all((s[0] == g[0]) for s, g in rec_)
        if cof:
            cnt_all += 1
            cnt_ok += all(o[0][i][0] == F(1, 4) for i in (0, 1))
    print(f'    命题 DG-1 的有限核验（d_x = d_y，前后严格同拍，初迹为 0）：情形 {cnt_all}，w₀₀、w₁₀ 保持基线者 {cnt_ok}')
    # (2) MINUS 经门控读权重
    o = as_lists(o26)
    oA = I_c(G, o, [((0, 0),)], F(0)); oB = o
    _, rA = G.step(as_lists(oA), ('A', (F(0), F(0))))
    _, rB = G.step(as_lists(oB), ('A', (F(0), F(0))))
    print('  (2) 抑制分项经门控读权重：拍 26 只改 w₀₀（z = 0 与 z = 0.418…），输入 0→输出 0 的抑制分项 P⁻ =',
          str(rA['comp'][(0, 0)][1]), '与', str(rB['comp'][(0, 0)][1]), '（同拍 g₀ 由 w 决定，y′₀ = d·y₀ + g₀）')
    # (3) 夹限与 δmax：饱和使 D 在 W 内消失
    o_sat = ([[F(1, 4) + F(6, 5), F(1, 4) + F(9, 10)], [F(1, 4) + F(6, 5), F(1, 4) + F(9, 10)], [F(1, 4)] * 2, [F(1, 4)] * 2],
             [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)] * 4, [F(0), F(0)])
    P = [('A', (0, 0))] * 30
    oe, rec_ = run_rec(G, o_sat, P)
    print('  (3) 夹限：起点分叉路深', [str(d) for d in depths(G, o_sat, K_FORK)], '，A×30（可塑性开）后路深', [str(d) for d in depths(G, oe, K_FORK)],
          '——两路都到 δmax = 7/4，D 消失')
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o_sat, K_FORK, P, 'fork')
    print('    该窗上首批开放读数 B 对一切合法 m 为假：', summarize(pieces, 'first')['all_false'], '；份额读数 B 对一切合法 m 为真：',
          summarize(pieces, 'share')['all_true'])
    print('十一、回写规则对 s1 结果的影响（R-均 与 R-移）')
    oo = as_lists(o26)
    unif = []
    for t, sig in enumerate(WIN):
        if t % 6 == 0:
            unif.append((26 + t, oo[0][0][0] == oo[0][1][0], oo[0][0][1] == oo[0][1][1], oo[0][2][0] == oo[0][3][0]))
        oo, _ = G.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
    print('  实际运行各干预点（W 起点与子段起点）的路内均匀性 (拍, w00 = w10, w01 = w11, w20 = w30)：', unif)
    for K, reading, nm in ((K_FORK, 'fork', '分叉'), (K_CONV, 'conv', '汇聚'), (K_SINGLE, 'single', '单突触')):
        _, pu, _ = param_B(G, o26, K, WIN, reading, rule='uniform')
        _, ps, _ = param_B(G, o26, K, WIN, reading, rule='shift')
        same = [(a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3]) for a in pu] == [(a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3]) for a in ps]
        print(f'  {nm}：R-均 与 R-移 的全部分段与判值逐项相同 {same}')
    print('  结论：s1 各干预点路内均匀，R-均 = R-移，s1 的各项读数与回写规则的选取无关。')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9　regress12.py　边界例与定向核验：定理 AGG、卷积式与有效弛豫率、命题 B-D、合法参数域、定理 S-INV 的前件、读数等价、引理 C-REP（10.1、10.2、10.3、10.6、10.9、11.1、11.7）

~~~python
"""《沉积动力具化数理式》附录程序·边界例与定向核验：回写集唯一性的两类判据、S-FORK 卷积式与有效弛豫率、等深恒等与 B ⇒ D、R-移 的合法参数域、
定理 S-INV 的前件（路互不重叠、超基线量）、读数等价、分叉读法的 C₌ 形式等价表示。
运行：python3 regress12.py
依赖：gate_class.py（附录 A.2，载入前核 SHA-256）。只用标准库，精确有理运算。
"""
import sys, os, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from deps12 import GC
import agg12 as AG
Z = F(0)

def u_rho(opens, lam, rho):
    """u_A^ρ(t) = Σ_{s=t₀}^{t} λ^{t−s}(1−ρ)^{s−t₀}[A_in 在 s 拍开]（0⁰ = 1）。opens 为 t₀…t 各拍的开闭。调用时传 ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]。"""
    t = len(opens) - 1
    return sum((lam ** (t - s) * (1 - rho) ** s for s in range(t + 1) if opens[s]), Z)

def gate(n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi):
    return GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, [lam] * n_out, [theta] * n_out, [Z] * n_out, [dx] * n_in, [dy] * n_out, Ap, Am, rho,
                   [[b] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)], [[lo] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)], [[hi] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)])

def first_open(G, recs, A_in):
    for t, r in enumerate(recs):
        if all(r['s'][i] for i in A_in):
            opened = frozenset(j for j in range(G.n_out) if r['g'][j])
            if opened:
                return opened
    return frozenset()

def run(G, o, hist):
    recs = []
    for sig in hist:
        o, r = G.step(o, sig)
        recs.append(r)
    return o, recs

def rd01():
    print('一、回写集唯一性（定理 AGG）')
    # （一）线性：盒 [0,1]^3，A = [(1,0,0),(0,1/2,1/2)]，c* = 0
    A = [[F(1), Z, Z], [Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]]
    r = AG.linear_writeback(A, F(0), [F(0)] * 3, [F(1)] * 3)
    print(f'  （一）线性边界例：rank A = {AG.rank(A)} < 3；回写集各坐标投影 {r["proj"]}；有解 {r["feasible"]}，唯一 {r["unique"]}；'
          f'仅用秩判据（rank = |X_K|）给出唯一 = {AG.rank(A) == 3}（不符）；可行方向判据给出唯一 = {r["cone_unique"]}')
    # （三）瓶颈：K = {{x},{y},{x,y}}，c* = 1/2，盒 [0,1]^2
    paths = [(0,), (1,), (0, 1)]
    for c in (F(1, 2), F(1)):
        u = AG.bottleneck_unique(paths, 2, c, F(1))
        b = AG.bottleneck_unique_brute(paths, 2, c, F(1))
        print(f'  （三）瓶颈边界例 K = {{x}},{{y}},{{x,y}}，c* = {c}，h = 1：逐路判据唯一 = {u}；子集穷举唯一 = {b}；「有长 ≥ 2 的路即不唯一」给出 不唯一（不符）')
    print('  修复后判据的有限核验：')
    n, viol, nint, vint = AG.random_linear_check(2000, seed=20261102)
    print(f'    线性：随机 A、c*、盒 {n} 例，「回写集为单点」（Fourier–Motzkin 逐坐标投影）与「ker A ∩ 盒切锥 = {{0}}」一致，违例 {viol}；'
          f'其中 c* 严格居盒内部且均匀解可行的 {nint} 例上与「rank A = |X_K|」一致，违例 {vint}')
    n, viol = AG.random_bottleneck_check(3000, seed=7)
    print(f'    瓶颈：随机路族 {n} 例，「逐路判据」与「升高位子集穷举」一致，违例 {viol}')

def rd02():
    print('二、S-FORK 的卷积式（有效弛豫率 ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]）')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, Z, F(2), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    o = ([[F(1), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
    o1, r1 = G.step(o, ('none', (Z, Z)))
    o2, r2 = G.step(o1, ('A', (Z, Z)))
    dvt = r2['vt'][0] - r2['vt'][1]
    E0 = o[0][0][0] - o[0][0][1]
    old = E0 * 1                                 # 略去弛豫的式：u_A = Σ λ^{t−s}[A 开] = 1
    new = E0 * u_rho([0, 1], Z, F(1, 2))      # 卷积式：u_A^ρ = Σ λ^{t−s}(1−ρ_eff)^{s−t₀}[A 开]
    print(f'  边界例（ρ = 1/2，plastic 开）：静置后权重 {[str(w) for w in o1[0][0]]}；第二拍 vt 差（实测）= {dvt}；略去弛豫的式 = {old}（不符）；卷积式 = {new}；门控 {r1["g"]}、{r2["g"]}')
    print('  定向回归：随机 SYM 状态（含路内不均）、ENT、无门控事件且无夹限的前缀上，plastic、bap 随机，逐拍核对 vt₀ − vt₁ = E(t₀)·u_A^{ρ_eff}(t)，ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]')
    rng = random.Random(11)
    tot = viol = 0
    per = {}
    wrong_hits = [0]
    for trial in range(1500):
        rho = rng.choice([Z, F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 3), F(3, 4), F(1, 10)])
        lam = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        d = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)])
        Am = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
        G = gate(3, 2, {'A': (1, 1, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0)}, lam, F(100), d, d, F(1, 8), Am, rho,
                 F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
        w = [[F(rng.randint(5, 35), 20) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(2)]
        wb = F(rng.randint(0, 20), 20)
        w.append([wb, wb])
        vv, yy = F(rng.randint(0, 5), 10), F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        o = (w, [vv, vv], [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4) for _ in range(3)], [yy, yy])
        E0 = (w[0][0] + w[1][0]) - (w[0][1] + w[1][1])
        plastic, bap = rng.choice([True, False]), rng.choice([True, False])
        rho_eff = rho if plastic else Z
        L = rng.randint(1, 8)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(L)]
        opens = []
        for t, sig in enumerate(hist):
            o, r = G.step(o, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            opens.append(1 if all(r['s'][i] for i in (0, 1)) else 0)
            if any(r['g']) or any(r['comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for k in r['comp']):
                break
            pred = E0 * u_rho(opens, lam, rho_eff)
            ok = (r['vt'][0] - r['vt'][1]) == pred
            tot += 1; viol += (not ok)
            key = (('p' if plastic else '-') + ('b' if bap else '-'), str(rho))
            per[key] = per.get(key, 0) + 1
            # 对照：误用 ρ（不乘 [plastic]）的公式
            if not plastic and rho != 0 and E0 != 0 and sum(opens[1:]) > 0:
                wrong_hits[0] += ((r['vt'][0] - r['vt'][1]) != E0 * u_rho(opens, lam, rho))
    byc = {}
    for (c, r), n in per.items():
        byc[c] = byc.get(c, 0) + n
    print(f'    拍数 {tot}（按 plastic/bap：{byc}；ρ 值 {sorted(set(r for _, r in per))} 各组均出现），违例 {viol}')
    print(f'    对照：plastic 关、ρ ≠ 0 时若以 ρ 代 ρ_eff，与实测不符的拍数 {wrong_hits[0]}')
    print('  端点：ρ = 1 时第一拍弛豫即抹去路深差，u_A^ρ 只剩 s = t₀ 一项：')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, F(1, 2), F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    for hist in ([('none', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))], [('A', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))]):
        o = ([[F(3, 4), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
        _, recs = run(G, o, hist)
        print(f'    权重 (3/4, 1/2)、史 {[h[0] for h in hist]}：各拍 vt 差 {[str(r["vt"][0] - r["vt"][1]) for r in recs]}')

def rd03():
    print('三、等深而路内不均：R-均 下 B ⇒ D 失败；等深恒等的 R-移 下成立')
    G = gate(2, 2, {'A': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}, Z, F(1, 4), F(1), F(1), Z, F(1, 2), Z, F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    w = [[Z, F(1, 2)], [F(1), F(1, 2)]]
    o = (w, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [F(1), F(1)])
    K = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))]
    dep = [sum((w[i][j] - F(1, 4) for (i, j) in p), Z) / 2 for p in K]
    hist = [('A', (F(-2), F(-2))), ('A', (Z, Z))]
    _, r0 = run(G, o, hist)
    Y0 = first_open(G, r0, (0, 1))
    c = dep[0]
    wu = [[F(1, 4) + c] * 2 for _ in range(2)]                     # R-均
    _, ru = run(G, (wu, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [F(1), F(1)]), hist)
    Yu = first_open(G, ru, (0, 1))
    ws = [[w[i][j] + (c - dep[[0, 1][j]]) for j in range(2)] for i in range(2)]   # R-移：路内各位平移同一量
    _, rs = run(G, (ws, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [F(1), F(1)]), hist)
    Ys = first_open(G, rs, (0, 1))
    print(f'  边界例：路深 {[str(x) for x in dep]}（D = 0），c* = {c}')
    print(f'    实际门控 {[r["g"] for r in r0]}，Y = {set(Y0)}；R-均 回写 {[[str(x) for x in r] for r in wu]} 后门控 {[r["g"] for r in ru]}，Y′ = {set(Yu)}，B = {Y0 != Yu}（B ⇒ D 失败）')
    print(f'    首拍夹限：实际运行中 w00 的夹前和 = {r0[0]["comp"][(0, 0)][3]}（< lo = 0，被截）——S-INV 前件「无夹限」不成立')
    print(f'    R-移 回写 {[[str(x) for x in r] for r in ws]}（等深时为恒等）后门控 {[r["g"] for r in rs]}，Y′ = {set(Ys)}，B = {Y0 != Ys}')
    print('  定向回归：随机 𝒦_g、随机路内不均而等深的状态、随机协议（含夹限）：')
    rng = random.Random(3)
    n = viol_shift = fail_u = fail_u_noclip = 0
    for trial in range(3000):
        lam = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(3, 4)])
        G = gate(2, 2, {'A': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}, lam, rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]), rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)]),
                 rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 8)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]),
                 rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
        s = F(rng.randint(0, 30), 20)                    # 每路突触和
        a0, a1 = F(rng.randint(0, 20), 20), F(rng.randint(0, 20), 20)
        if not (0 <= a0 <= min(s, 2) and 0 <= s - a0 <= 2 and 0 <= a1 <= min(s, 2) and 0 <= s - a1 <= 2):
            continue
        w = [[a0, a1], [s - a0, s - a1]]
        y = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        o = (w, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [y, y])
        hist = [('A' if rng.random() < .7 else 'none', (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([Z, F(-1), F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 5))]
        _, r0 = run(G, o, hist)
        Y0 = first_open(G, r0, (0, 1))
        c = s / 2 - F(1, 4)
        wu = [[F(1, 4) + c] * 2 for _ in range(2)]
        _, ru = run(G, (wu, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [y, y]), hist)
        clip = any(r['comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for r in r0 + ru for k in r['comp'])
        n += 1
        if first_open(G, ru, (0, 1)) != Y0:
            fail_u += 1
            fail_u_noclip += (not clip)
        # R-移：等深时回写恒等，重跑与实际相同
        _, rs = run(G, o, hist)
        viol_shift += (first_open(G, rs, (0, 1)) != Y0)
    print(f'    情形 {n}：R-均 下 B ⇒ D 失败 {fail_u} 例（其中两次运行都无夹限者 {fail_u_noclip}，应为 0：S-INV）；R-移 下失败 {viol_shift}')

def rd04():
    print('四、可塑性关闭时弛豫不运行（ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]）')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, Z, F(2), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    for plastic in (True, False):
        for bap in (True, False):
            o = ([[F(1), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
            o1, r1 = G.step(o, ('none', (Z, Z)), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            o2, r2 = G.step(o1, ('A', (Z, Z)), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            rho_eff = F(1, 2) if plastic else Z
            print(f'  plastic={plastic}, bap={bap}：静置后权重 {[str(x) for x in o1[0][0]]}；第二拍 vt 差（实测）{r2["vt"][0] - r2["vt"][1]}；'
                  f'ρ_eff 式 {F(1, 2) * u_rho([0, 1], Z, rho_eff)}；以 ρ 代 ρ_eff 之式 {F(1, 2) * u_rho([0, 1], Z, F(1, 2))}')
    print('  端点「ρ = 1 首拍抹平」的前件须含 plastic = True（bap 无关）：')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, F(1, 2), F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    for plastic, bap in ((True, True), (True, False), (False, True)):
        o = ([[F(3, 4), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
        oo, rr = o, []
        for sig in [('none', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))]:
            oo, r = G.step(oo, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            rr.append(r)
        print(f'    ρ = 1、权重 (3/4, 1/2)、史 none, A、plastic={plastic}、bap={bap}：各拍 vt 差 {[str(r["vt"][0] - r["vt"][1]) for r in rr]}')

def rd05():
    print('五、R-移 的合法参数域')
    import snn12 as N
    G = GC.snn_gate(N.S)
    def st(w00, w10):
        w = [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        w[0][0], w[1][0] = w00, w10
        return (w, [Z, Z], [Z] * 4, [Z, Z])
    H1 = [('A', (0, 0))]
    o = st(Z, F(2))
    dom = N.legal_domain(G, o, N.K_FORK, 'shift')
    print(f'  退化例：w00 = 0、w10 = 2，路深 {[str(d) for d in N.depths(G, o, N.K_FORK)]}；R-移 合法域 ∩ [min, max] = {dom}')
    for c in (Z, F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        print(f'    c = {c}：I_c 有定义 {N.I_c(G, o, N.K_FORK, c, "shift") is not None}')
    try:
        N.param_explore(G, o, N.K_FORK, H1, Z, F(3, 4), rule='shift')
        print('    参数机在 (0, 3/4) 上运行（错误）')
    except ValueError as e:
        print('    参数机在 (0, 3/4) 上拒绝运行：', e)
    Y0, pcs, bad = N.param_B(G, o, N.K_FORK, H1, 'fork', rule='shift')
    print('    param_B 分段：', [(k, str(a), str(b), 'undef-Im' if y is None else 'defined') for k, a, b, v, y in pcs])
    o = st(F(1, 2), F(3, 2))
    dom = N.legal_domain(G, o, N.K_FORK, 'shift')
    Y0, pcs, bad = N.param_B(G, o, N.K_FORK, H1 * 3, 'fork', rule='shift')
    print(f'  非退化合法域：w00 = 1/2、w10 = 3/2，路深 {[str(d) for d in N.depths(G, o, N.K_FORK)]}，合法域 {tuple(str(x) for x in dom)}')
    for c in (Z, F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        pc = N.piece_at(pcs, c)
        print(f'    c = {c}：I_c 有定义 {N.I_c(G, o, N.K_FORK, c, "shift") is not None}；参数机段 {pc[0]}，{"undef-Im" if pc[4] is None else "B=" + str(pc[3]["first"])}')
    print(f'    m 函数合法而 I_m 未定义：m = min（c* = 0）；m = max（c* = 3/4）有定义')
    K_sh = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 0),)]
    print(f'  共享结构位：K = {{路 A, 输入 0 支}}，R-移：合法域 = {N.legal_domain(G, o, K_sh, "shift")}；I_c(1/4) 有定义 {N.I_c(G, o, K_sh, F(1, 4), "shift") is not None}；'
          f'param_B 全部 undef-Im {all(p[4] is None for p in N.param_B(G, o, K_sh, H1, "fork", rule="shift")[1])}')
    print('  随机回归：随机路内不均状态、随机短史；在 min、max、合法域两端、域内随机点、域外紧邻点上比对 I_c 有定义性与参数机分段，并比对有定义点的记录')
    rng = random.Random(23)
    n = viol = notall = empty = pts = 0
    for trial in range(400):
        w = [[F(rng.randint(0, 40), 20) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        o = (w, [F(rng.randint(0, 5), 10)] * 2, [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4) for _ in range(4)], [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2)
        K = rng.choice([N.K_FORK, N.K_CONV])
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (0, 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))]
        rd = 'fork' if K is N.K_FORK else 'conv'
        Y0, pcs, bad = N.param_B(G, o, K, hist, rd, rule='shift', verify=1, rng=rng)
        viol += bad
        ds = N.depths(G, o, K); lo, hi = min(ds), max(ds)
        dom = N.legal_domain(G, o, K, 'shift')
        n += 1
        if dom is None:
            empty += 1
        elif not (dom[0] == lo and dom[1] == hi):
            notall += 1
        cs = {lo, hi}
        if dom not in (None, 'reject'):
            cs |= {dom[0], dom[1], dom[0] + (dom[1] - dom[0]) * F(rng.randint(1, 99), 100)}
            for e in (dom[0] - F(1, 1000), dom[1] + F(1, 1000)):
                if lo <= e <= hi:
                    cs.add(e)
        for c in cs:
            pts += 1
            pc = N.piece_at(pcs, c)
            Ic = N.I_c(G, o, K, c, 'shift')
            if pc is None or ((pc[4] is None) != (Ic is None)):
                viol += 1
            elif Ic is not None and N.Ys(N.run_rec(G, Ic, hist)[1], rd) != pc[4]:
                viol += 1
    print(f'    状态 {n}，检查点 {pts}；合法域为空 {empty}、合法域真包含于 [min, max]（存在合法 m 使 I_m 未定义）{notall}；不一致 {viol}')

def rd06():
    print('六、定理 S-INV 的前件：路互不重叠、只改这些结构位、其余起点状态相同；超基线量之差按 (1 − ρ_eff) 缩减')
    # 重叠入口组：各路和相等而门控不同（被「路互不重叠」前件排除）
    G = GC.Gate(3, 1, {'none': (0, 0, 0), 'A': (1, 1, 1)}, [Z], [F(8, 5)], [Z], [F(1, 2)] * 3, [F(1, 2)], Z, Z, Z,
                [[Z] for _ in range(3)], [[Z] for _ in range(3)], [[F(2)] for _ in range(3)])
    K = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((1, 0), (2, 0))]
    a = G.init(w=[[F(1, 2)], [F(1, 2)], [F(1, 2)]])
    b = G.init(w=[[F(3, 4)], [F(1, 4)], [F(3, 4)]])
    sums = [sum((o[0][i][j] for i, j in p), Z) for p in K for o in (a, b)]
    _, ra = G.step(a, ('A', (Z,)), plastic=False)
    _, rb = G.step(b, ('A', (Z,)), plastic=False)
    print(f'  重叠入口组（两路共用 w10）：两状态各路和 {[str(x) for x in sums]}；门控实参 {ra["vt"][0]} 与 {rb["vt"][0]}，门控 {ra["g"]} 与 {rb["g"]}——不满足「路互不重叠」，结论不成立')
    # 组内基线不同：权重之差不按比例，超基线量之差按比例
    G = GC.Gate(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'A': (1, 1)}, [Z], [F(10)], [Z], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], Z, Z, F(1, 2),
                [[F(1, 4)], [F(1, 2)]], [[Z]] * 2, [[F(2)]] * 2)
    o = G.init(w=[[F(1)], [F(1)]])
    o2, _ = G.step(o, ('none', (Z,)))
    dw0, dw1 = o[0][0][0] - o[0][1][0], o2[0][0][0] - o2[0][1][0]
    de0 = (o[0][0][0] - G.b[0][0]) - (o[0][1][0] - G.b[1][0])
    de1 = (o2[0][0][0] - G.b[0][0]) - (o2[0][1][0] - G.b[1][0])
    print(f'  组内基线 (1/4, 1/2)、权重 (1, 1)、ρ = 1/2、plastic 开：权重差 {dw0} → {dw1}；超基线量之差 {de0} → {de1}（= (1 − ρ_eff)·{de0}）')
    # 随机核验：互不重叠的入口组路、组内基线可不同、plastic × bap × ρ
    rng = random.Random(29)
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    K = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((2, 0), (3, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))]
    n = viol = clipped = 0
    per = {}
    for trial in range(1500):
        rho = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 10), F(1, 3), F(1, 2), F(1)])
        plastic, bap = rng.choice([True, False]), rng.choice([True, False])
        b = [[F(rng.randint(0, 8), 16) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(4, 2, pat, [rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(3, 4)])] * 2, [rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)])] * 2, [Z] * 2,
                    [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, rng.choice([Z, F(1, 8)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 16)]), rho, b,
                    [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
        w = [[F(rng.randint(4, 28), 16) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        w2 = [r[:] for r in w]
        for path in K:
            (i1, j1), (i2, j2) = path
            d = F(rng.randint(-6, 6), 16)
            w2[i1][j1] += d; w2[i2][j2] -= d
        if not all(0 <= w2[i][j] <= 2 for i in range(4) for j in range(2)):
            continue
        x = [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2 + [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2
        common = ([F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8)] * 2, x, [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2)
        oa, ob = (w, *common), (w2, *[list(c) for c in common])
        hist = [(rng.choice(list(pat)), (rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 6))]
        clip = False
        same = True
        for sig in hist:
            oa, r1 = G.step(oa, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            ob, r2 = G.step(ob, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            clip = clip or any(r[ 'comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for r in (r1, r2) for path in K for k in path)
            same = same and r1['g'] == r2['g'] and oa[1:] == ob[1:] and \
                all(sum((oa[0][i][j] for i, j in p), Z) == sum((ob[0][i][j] for i, j in p), Z) for p in K)
        if clip:
            clipped += 1
            continue
        n += 1
        key = ('plastic' if plastic else '-') + '/' + ('bap' if bap else '-') + '/' + ('ρ_eff>0' if plastic and rho else 'ρ_eff=0')
        per[key] = per.get(key, 0) + 1
        viol += (not same)
    print(f'  随机核验：路互不重叠（含组内基线不同）、只改 K、其余起点相同、无夹限的 {n} 例（按开关与 ρ_eff：{per}；有夹限而略去 {clipped}），门控、v、x、y 与各路和逐拍不一致者 {viol}')

def rd07():
    print('七、等深恒等、辅助操作与读数等价')
    G = GC.Gate(2, 2, {'none': (0, 0), 'A': (1, 1)}, [Z] * 2, [F(1)] * 2, [Z] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2, Z, Z, Z,
                [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(2)], [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(2)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(2)])
    a = G.init(w=[[F(1, 4), F(1, 2)], [F(3, 4), F(1, 2)]])
    b = G.init(w=[[F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2])
    ga, gb = [], []
    for sig in [('A', (Z, Z)), ('none', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))]:
        a, ra = G.step(a, sig, plastic=False); b, rb = G.step(b, sig, plastic=False)
        ga.append(ra['g']); gb.append(rb['g'])
    print(f'  等深而路内不均（权重按入口行 ((1/4, 1/2), (3/4, 1/2))，两路和均为 1，D = 0）：R-均 把四个权重都改为 1/2（状态改变，非恒等）；'
          f'固定权重下门控记录 {ga} 与 {gb} 相同 {ga == gb}——读数等价的正例；它不使 R-均 成为合法回写')

def rd08():
    print('八、分叉读法的 C₌ 等价表示：p̂(t) = −min_{s≤t} σ_s，ĝ(S) = −S（σ_s 为拍 s 上使门控实参达阈的路和下界）')
    INF = None
    def sig_of(a, u, th):
        if u > 0:
            return (th - a) / u
        return '-inf' if a >= th else '+inf'
    def le(x, y):          # x ≤ y，x、y ∈ ℚ ∪ {±inf}
        if x == '-inf' or y == '+inf':
            return True
        if x == '+inf' or y == '-inf':
            return False
        return x <= y
    def mn(x, y):
        return x if le(x, y) else y
    # 驱动下降的例：λ = 1/2、θ = 10、史 A, none, none
    G = gate(2, 2, {'A': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}, F(1, 2), F(10), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, Z, F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    us = [u_rho([1], F(1, 2), Z), u_rho([1, 0], F(1, 2), Z), u_rho([1, 0, 0], F(1, 2), Z)]
    print(f'  例：u_A 依次 {[str(x) for x in us]}（下降）；所构造的 p̂ 由前缀最小值定义，按定义非减')
    rng = random.Random(31)
    n = viol = 0
    mono_fail = 0
    for trial in range(1500):
        lam = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        rho = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 3), F(1, 2), F(1)])
        plastic, bap = rng.choice([True, False]), rng.choice([True, False])
        Am = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 16)])
        th = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)])
        def mk(theta):
            return GC.Gate(3, 2, {'A': (1, 1, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0)}, [lam] * 2, [theta] * 2, [Z] * 2,
                           [F(1, 2)] * 3, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), Am, rho, [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(3)],
                           [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(3)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(3)])
        G, Gi = mk(th), mk(F(10 ** 6))
        S = [F(rng.randint(4, 40), 20), F(rng.randint(4, 40), 20)]
        if S[0] == S[1]:
            continue
        u0 = F(rng.randint(0, 6), 20)
        wb = F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20)
        w = [[S[0] / 2 + u0, S[1] / 2 + u0], [S[0] / 2 - u0, S[1] / 2 - u0], [wb, wb]]
        if not all(0 <= w[i][j] <= 2 for i in range(3) for j in range(2)):
            continue
        vv, yy = F(rng.randint(0, 5), 10), F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)
        o = (w, [vv, vv], [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2 + [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)], [yy, yy])
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([Z, F(1, 4)]))) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 8))]
        # 无门控的同参数运行给出 a(s)、u(s)；检查 K 内无夹限
        oi, clip, ab = [r[:] for r in o[0]], False, []
        oi = (oi, list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3]))
        for sig in hist:
            oi, ri = Gi.step(oi, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            clip = clip or any(ri['comp'][(i, j)][3] != ri['comp'][(i, j)][4] for i in (0, 1) for j in (0, 1))
            u = (ri['vt'][0] - ri['vt'][1]) / (S[0] - S[1])
            ab.append((ri['vt'][0] - u * S[0], u))
        if clip:
            continue
        sig_min, sigs = '+inf', []
        for (a_, u_) in ab:
            sig_min = mn(sig_min, sig_of(a_, u_, th))
            sigs.append(sig_min)
        mono_fail += any(not le(sigs[k + 1], sigs[k]) for k in range(len(sigs) - 1))
        # 实际运行：各输出的首个门控拍
        oo, tau = (o[0], list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3])), [None, None]
        for t, sig in enumerate(hist):
            oo, r = G.step(oo, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            for j in (0, 1):
                if r['g'][j] and tau[j] is None:
                    tau[j] = t
        pred = [next((t for t, sg in enumerate(sigs) if le(sg, S[j])), None) for j in (0, 1)]
        n += 1
        viol += (pred != tau)
    print(f'  随机核验（SYM、ENT、K 内无夹限；ρ、plastic、bap 随机）{n} 例：σ 的前缀最小值非增违例 {mono_fail}；'
          f'「输出 j 的首个门控拍 = min{{t：p̂(t) ≥ ĝ(S_j)}}」不一致 {viol}')

def main():
    rd01()
    rd02()
    rd03()
    rd04()
    rd05()
    rd06()
    rd07()
    rd08()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

二、边界例与判据核验  
&nbsp;&nbsp;线性：A = \[(1,0,0),(0,1/2,1/2)\]，c\* = 0，盒 \[0,1\]³：投影 \[('0', '0'), ('0', '0'), ('0', '0')\]，唯一 True，切锥判据 True，rank 2

&nbsp;&nbsp;瓶颈：K = {x},{y},{x,y}，c\* = 1：逐路判据唯一 True，穷举 True

十一、回写规则对 s1 结果的影响（R-均 与 R-移）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;实际运行各干预点（W 起点与子段起点）的路内均匀性 (拍, w00 = w10, w01 = w11, w20 = w30)： \[(26, True, True, True), (32, True, True, True), (38, True, True, True)\]  
&nbsp;&nbsp;分叉：R-均 与 R-移 的全部分段与判值逐项相同 True  
&nbsp;&nbsp;汇聚：R-均 与 R-移 的全部分段与判值逐项相同 True  
&nbsp;&nbsp;单突触：R-均 与 R-移 的全部分段与判值逐项相同 True  
&nbsp;&nbsp;结论：s1 各干预点路内均匀，R-均 = R-移，s1 的各项读数与回写规则的选取无关。  
B.6　运行命令：python3 regress12.py  
一、回写集唯一性（定理 AGG）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;（一）线性边界例：rank A = 2 \< 3；回写集各坐标投影 \[('0', '0'), ('0', '0'), ('0', '0')\]；有解 True，唯一 True；仅用秩判据（rank = \|X\_K\|）给出唯一 = False（不符）；可行方向判据给出唯一 = True  
&nbsp;&nbsp;（三）瓶颈边界例 K = {x},{y},{x,y}，c\* = 1/2，h = 1：逐路判据唯一 = True；子集穷举唯一 = True；「有长 ≥ 2 的路即不唯一」给出 不唯一（不符）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;（三）瓶颈边界例 K = {x},{y},{x,y}，c\* = 1，h = 1：逐路判据唯一 = True；子集穷举唯一 = True；「有长 ≥ 2 的路即不唯一」给出 不唯一（不符）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;修复后判据的有限核验：  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;线性：随机 A、c\*、盒 1622 例，「回写集为单点」（Fourier–Motzkin 逐坐标投影）与「ker A ∩ 盒切锥 = {0}」一致，违例 0；其中 c\* 严格居盒内部且均匀解可行的 518 例上与「rank A = \|X\_K\|」一致，违例 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;瓶颈：随机路族 3000 例，「逐路判据」与「升高位子集穷举」一致，违例 0  
二、S-FORK 的卷积式（有效弛豫率 ρ\_eff = ρ·\[plastic\]）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;边界例（ρ = 1/2，plastic 开）：静置后权重 \['5/8', '3/8'\]；第二拍 vt 差（实测）= 1/4；略去弛豫的式 = 1/2（不符）；卷积式 = 1/4；门控 (0, 0)、(0, 0)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;定向回归：随机 SYM 状态（含路内不均）、ENT、无门控事件且无夹限的前缀上，plastic、bap 随机，逐拍核对 vt₀ − vt₁ = E(t₀)·u\_A^{ρ\_eff}(t)，ρ\_eff = ρ·\[plastic\]  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;拍数 6810（按 plastic/bap：{'-b': 1683, 'p-': 1650, 'pb': 1784, '--': 1693}；ρ 值 \['0', '1', '1/10', '1/2', '1/3', '3/4'\] 各组均出现），违例 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;对照：plastic 关、ρ ≠ 0 时若以 ρ 代 ρ\_eff，与实测不符的拍数 1548  
&nbsp;&nbsp;端点：ρ = 1 时第一拍弛豫即抹去路深差，u\_A^ρ 只剩 s = t₀ 一项：  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;权重 (3/4, 1/2)、史 \['none', 'A'\]：各拍 vt 差 \['0', '0'\]  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;权重 (3/4, 1/2)、史 \['A', 'A'\]：各拍 vt 差 \['1/4', '1/8'\]  
三、等深而路内不均：R-均 下 B ⇒ D 失败；等深恒等的 R-移 下成立  
&nbsp;&nbsp;边界例：路深 \['1/4', '1/4'\]（D = 0），c\* = 1/4  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;实际门控 \[(0, 0), (1, 0)\]，Y = {0}；R-均 回写 \[\['1/2', '1/2'\], \['1/2', '1/2'\]\] 后门控 \[(0, 0), (0, 0)\]，Y′ = set()，B = True（B ⇒ D 失败）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;首拍夹限：实际运行中 w00 的夹前和 = -1/2（\< lo = 0，被截）——S-INV 前件「无夹限」不成立  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;R-移 回写 \[\['0', '1/2'\], \['1', '1/2'\]\]（等深时为恒等）后门控 \[(0, 0), (1, 0)\]，Y′ = {0}，B = False

四、可塑性关闭时弛豫不运行（ρ\_eff = ρ·\[plastic\]）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;plastic=True, bap=True：静置后权重 \['5/8', '3/8'\]；第二拍 vt 差（实测）1/4；ρ\_eff 式 1/4；以 ρ 代 ρ\_eff 之式 1/4

&nbsp;&nbsp;plastic=False, bap=True：静置后权重 \['1', '1/2'\]；第二拍 vt 差（实测）1/2；ρ\_eff 式 1/2；以 ρ 代 ρ\_eff 之式 1/4

&nbsp;&nbsp;端点「ρ = 1 首拍抹平」的前件须含 plastic = True（bap 无关）：  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ρ = 1、权重 (3/4, 1/2)、史 none, A、plastic=True、bap=True：各拍 vt 差 \['0', '0'\]

&nbsp;&nbsp;非退化合法域：w00 = 1/2、w10 = 3/2，路深 \['3/4', '0'\]，合法域 ('1/4', '3/4')

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;m 函数合法而 I\_m 未定义：m = min（c\* = 0）；m = max（c\* = 3/4）有定义  
&nbsp;&nbsp;共享结构位：K = {路 A, 输入 0 支}，R-移：合法域 = reject；I\_c(1/4) 有定义 False；param\_B 全部 undef-Im True  
&nbsp;&nbsp;随机回归：随机路内不均状态、随机短史；在 min、max、合法域两端、域内随机点、域外紧邻点上比对 I\_c 有定义性与参数机分段，并比对有定义点的记录

六、定理 S-INV 的前件：路互不重叠、只改这些结构位、其余起点状态相同；超基线量之差按 (1 − ρ\_eff) 缩减  
&nbsp;&nbsp;重叠入口组（两路共用 w10）：两状态各路和 \['1', '1', '1', '1'\]；门控实参 3/2 与 7/4，门控 (0,) 与 (1,)——不满足「路互不重叠」，结论不成立  
&nbsp;&nbsp;组内基线 (1/4, 1/2)、权重 (1, 1)、ρ = 1/2、plastic 开：权重差 0 → -1/8；超基线量之差 1/4 → 1/8（= (1 − ρ\_eff)·1/4）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;随机核验：路互不重叠（含组内基线不同）、只改 K、其余起点相同、无夹限的 1276 例（按开关与 ρ\_eff：{'plastic/-/ρ\_eff\>0': 272, '-/bap/ρ\_eff=0': 308, 'plastic/-/ρ\_eff=0': 69, 'plastic/bap/ρ\_eff\>0': 233, '-/-/ρ\_eff=0': 329, 'plastic/bap/ρ\_eff=0': 65}；有夹限而略去 55），门控、v、x、y 与各路和逐拍不一致者 0  
七、等深恒等、辅助操作与读数等价  
&nbsp;&nbsp;等深而路内不均（权重按入口行 ((1/4, 1/2), (3/4, 1/2))，两路和均为 1，D = 0）：R-均 把四个权重都改为 1/2（状态改变，非恒等）；固定权重下门控记录 \[(1, 1), (0, 0), (1, 1)\] 与 \[(1, 1), (0, 0), (1, 1)\] 相同 True——读数等价的正例；它不使 R-均 成为合法回写  
八、分叉读法的 C₌ 等价表示：p̂(t) = −min\_{s≤t} σ\_s，ĝ(S) = −S（σ\_s 为拍 s 上使门控实参达阈的路和下界）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;例：u\_A 依次 \['1', '1/2', '1/4'\]（下降）；所构造的 p̂ 由前缀最小值定义，按定义非减  
&nbsp;&nbsp;随机核验（SYM、ENT、K 内无夹限；ρ、plastic、bap 随机）1245 例：σ 的前缀最小值非增违例 0；「输出 j 的首个门控拍 = min{t：p̂(t) ≥ ĝ(S\_j)}」不一致 0
